## 7. URLs & Mmekọrịta Cloud

| Ngbanwe                                  | Ndabara                                                   | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL nke dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta ime ga-eji kpọọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL loopback/kọnteena ọbụlagodi mgbe a na-eji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dị ọhaneze.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(efu)_                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL endpoint nke cloud relay (atụmatụ akwụmụgwọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge njedebe HTTP maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                                    | Nhazi build Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge-build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul ndị nwere ikike pụrụ iche kpamkpam na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _.build/ mmepụta standalone_                              | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Ndochi oge-build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gachara na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(efu)_                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Nzuzo ekekọrịtara nke a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Cloud Sync dochie nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. A ga-amalite ịmanye ndabara a na v3.9.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                                   | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi na omume otu-usoro nke v3.8.5, "bubata ihe niile", na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ọhaneze maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọhaneze ewepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọhaneze kwụsiri ike mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị ewepụtara ga-eji host reverse-proxy canonical.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(efu)_                                                   | Akụkụ onye ahịa                                  | Oyiyi `CLOUD_URL` dị n'akụkụ onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                       | _(edobeghị — na-eji `PORT` dị ka ndabere)_                | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Port ndabere n'akụkụ klayenti maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin ọ bụla (SSR/nnwale); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(edobeghị)_                                              | Ihe na-achọpụta public origin, URL onyonyo       | Origin OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-eji ma nwee mkpa kachasị elu, nke a na-eji emepụta URL ọhaneze na iji nyochaa origin ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-enweta OmniRoute site na URL ime, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-enwerịrị ike ibudata mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọhaneze. Etinyekwala **`/v1`**.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru ezu nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye klayenti sidecar. Mgbe edobeghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị origin arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọhaneze na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edobeghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka header public origin ebufere. Edobeghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ebufere obi naanị site na proxy loopback nwere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa peer proxy private-LAN obi, ma na-enye bridge `API_PORT` ohere idowe `X-Forwarded-For` nke peer dị otú ahụ. N'ebe mmepụta, họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya.          |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọhaneze maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Override nwere mkpa kachasị elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. A na-eji ya dị ka ndabere mgbe edobeghị variable bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Public origin a na-eji ahazi URL callback asynchronous. Nhọrọ ndabere nwere mkpa kachasị ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dị ka URL ọhaneze izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint nyocha oke CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`          | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits` | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`           | Endpoint oke ClinePass nke awa 5 / kwa izu / kwa ọnwa nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                     | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API key na-eme nkwenye, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta header njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) na arịrịọ upstream opencode-go/zen nke klayenti ezipụghị, ka Cloudflare dị na egress VPS nwee ike ịnabata ha (#6210/#5997). Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara nke a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ ma enweghị nhazi `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` akọwapụtara maka onye na-eweta ọrụ ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị na ndị mmebe opencode. N’arịrịọ na-enweghị igodo nke sistemụ dị n’elu na-enyocha, a ga-eji ndabara a dochie uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` kama ịjụ ya.                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnyeisi `x-opencode-client` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnyeisi `x-opencode-project` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`     | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Otu ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị irè nke a na-ekewa ọdịda ha n’elu dịka IP ọpụpụ si dị (otu 429 na-adọba njikọ nwanne niile na-ekekọrịta IP ahụ). Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-eji ndabara ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ usoro ọdịda ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta e ji rikoma kewaa na-anọchi otu ahụ. A na-ekewa atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ n’elu dịka akaụntụ si dị, ya mere ha nwere ike ịgbanyụ ya n’enweghị nsogbu (#9611). |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`    | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imegharị ahụ arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo (akara nkwanye ozugbo na ndepụta ngwaọrụ). A ka na-etinye nkụnyeisi ndị ahụ. A na-agụ ya n’arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`   | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha ngwaọrụ e ji rikoma kewaa nke a ga-ekwupụta n’arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo nke na-enweghị nke ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ na-eji otu ngwaọrụ nwa oge nke a gwara model ahụ ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndenye na-ezighi ezi anya.                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud eji enweta oke ojiji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / ngwa nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Kuki `__Secure-session` nke Ollama Cloud eji enweta oke ojiji na peeji ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var kuki `__Secure-session` ọzọ nke Ollama Cloud. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var kuki `__Secure-session` ọzọ nke Ollama Cloud nke a na-eji tupu aha mkpirikpi ndị ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ mpaghara Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relays / ngwa nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye ya n’azụ proxy ntụgharị (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọhaneze gị kwụsiri ike (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ọhaneze emepụtara ga-eji aha nnabata ahụ. Ma ọ bụghị ya, OAuth callbacks nwere ike ịda n’ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba, ebe njikọ ọhaneze emepụtara nwere ike ịtụ aka na origin dị n’ime container.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL loopback/container dị n’ime maka ọrụ server-na-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ aha nnabata ọhaneze maka internal self-fetches ndị nwere ozi nzere.
>
> Dashboard writes ndị enyochala njirimara ha anaghị achọ static public base URL: dashboard ahụ na-eji session-bound CSRF token eziga same-origin unsafe requests. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta public-origin validation n’otu ebe maka njikọta ihe nchọgharị ndị na-abụghị nke dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị explicit public URL env vars obi; a na-eleghara raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` headers anya belụsọ ma agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma jiri token kaa immediate proxy peer ahụ akara dị ka nke a tụkwasịrị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie same-origin dashboard requests; CORS bụ naanị maka cross-origin browser clients.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Mee ka oku ndị na-aga na ndị na-eweta LLM dị n'elu gafee na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka ịchịkwa ọpụpụ, iduzi dịka mpaghara ala, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` na-eji `true` dịka ndabara. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ
`off` iji manye HTTP/1.1 na ndị Undici dispatcher dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: ọpụpụ ozugbo, proxy HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relay, na
nnwale ọhụrụ ha na socket ọhụrụ. Nke a na-echekwa nyocha asambodo TLS, njirimara
proxy, oke njikọ, na ntọala keep-alive. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe
ị gbanwechara ya n'ihi na a na-edobe ndị dispatcher na cache, a na-emepụtakwa ndị relay agent mgbe mmalite.
Mgbanwe a anaghị achịkwa wreq TLS fingerprinting ma ọ bụ usoro mbufe akọwapụtara maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------------------- | ---------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`     | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mee ka onye nnọchi anya SOCKS5 rụọ ọrụ maka oku upstream. Jiri `false` họrọ ịghara iji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`     | Akụkụ klayenti                                 | Nghọta dị n'akụkụ klayenti banyere nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`     | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara a na-agbanye na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nọchiri anya dị na DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ). Ọdọ proxy na ntụgharị nke akaụntụ ọ bụla na-akwụsị inyeghachi otu onye otu dara ugbu a (nnwale TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 e nwetara site na ya) ruo oge nke na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọdịda ahụ mere ọzọ, ruo n'ókè kachasị. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                                       |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(edoghị)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta ikike maka ebe njikwa-selector nke local-core gafere loopback (aha ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IP ndị e ji rikoma/ohere kewaa; `*` na-emepe LAN/nkeonwe mana ọ naghị emepe metadata/link-local ma ọlị). A na-ekwe loopback site na http/https na ndabara. Uru DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ karịa mgbanwe a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị oghere.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`   | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ, nke na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọ mere ọzọ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`   | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Ókè kachasị (ms) nke idowe 429 n'akụkụ. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe ókè kachasị dị ala karịa ntọala, ọ ga-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata ókè kachasị na-emekwa ka oge ichefu usoro ahụ dị mkpụmkpụ: a na-ehichapụ ndenye `2 x max` mgbe ha kubiri ume.                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`    | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Ọkọlọtọ njirimara a ga-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala nọchiri anya dị na DB dashboard ga-ebu ụzọ). Maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke a na-ekewa oke ha dịka adreesị ọpụpụ si dị, debe onye otu ọdọ nke na-eji otu adreesị ọpụpụ ahụrụ anya dị ka onye otu a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ozugbo n'okpuru ndị otu ahụ siri ike. Ọ bụ naanị nhazi n'usoro; anaghị ewepụ ya ma ọlị. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbaàmà ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`   | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Windo nlọghachi (ms) nke na-amachi mkpu webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye otu ọdọ ọ bụla ka ọtụtụ ọjụjụ n'otu ntabi anya zipụ naanị otu mmemme. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ bụla a na-ezipụ ya. E debere ókè ya na `1000..3600000`; uru gafere ókè ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara n'enweghị ọkwa.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTP maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTPS maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edoghị)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-ekedo ebe echo-IP nke nyocha ọpụpụ proxy na-eji n'otu URL. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-ebu ụzọ nwalee `api64.ipify.org`, mesịa `api4.ipify.org`, ka a ghara ịkọwa tunnel ndị na-akwado naanị IPv4 dị ka ndị nwụrụ anwụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edoghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Aha host/IP ndị e ji rikoma kewaa, nke a ga-agafe proxy maka ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`       | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ socket kachasị elu nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge maka dispatcher proxy HTTP/SOCKS ọ bụla echekwara na cache. Stream SSE ndị na-adịte aka, dịka Codex `/v1/responses`, chọrọ ihe karịrị otu njikọ mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-ekekọrịta otu proxy dị n'ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-amachi ụkpụrụ karịrị `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`    | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge nchere kachasị maka handshake (njikọ) SOCKS5 na ms. Bulie ya mgbe nnukwu ọrụ n'otu oge rutere otu host gateway residential (dịka arịrịọ 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafe 10s mgbe pool jupụtara, ọbụlagodi na enwere ike iru proxy ahụ; ma ọ bụghị ya, nke a ga-apụta n'ụzọ na-ezighi ezi dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. A na-amachi ya na `120000`.                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`    | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a na-**ajụ (fail-closed)** arịrịọ nke proxy e kenyere ya enweghị ike idozi, kama ịlaghachi na njikọ ozugbo — nke a na-egbochi mkpughe real-IP. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachi fallback DIRECT ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`    | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js mee ka fingerprint TLS yie nke ọzọ (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Nke a na-egbochi mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ndị provider a na-anabata maka routing TLS ọhụrụ site na proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`), nke e ji rikoma kewaa. TLS ozugbo na-edobe omume ochie ya mgbe edoghị ya; naanị provider ndị a ka a na-ebufe site na bridge fingerprint Chrome-124. A na-ewepụ Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edoghị allowlist ahụ, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-akpalite Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici nke sistemụ n'onwe ya na-erute API ahụ.                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`    | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka context browser Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie certificate HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Ọnọdụ eji eme ihe

| Ọnọdụ                                            | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na tunnel SSH**                    | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP nke ụlọ ọrụ**                       | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                          | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (gụnyere ya)                                                              |
| **Ọpụpụ a na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo**    | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrịọ ga-ada kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị, kama ikpughe site na njikọ ozugbo.      |
| **Usoro ochie / mmepe — kwe ka fallback ozugbo** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu e mesie nchekwa ike: a na-eji njikọ ozugbo mgbe idozi proxy dara.         |

> **Rịba ama (ịgafe nkwado NVIDIA — #3226):** Endpoint NVIDIA maka nkwado API-key
> na-akwụsị mgbe e zigara ya site na fetch proxy/TLS emezigharịrị nke zuru ụwa ọnụ (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` kpachaara anya na-agafe
> patch proxy maka naanị oku nkwado ahụ site n'iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ ihe pụrụ iche edepụtara nke oke ya doro anya — ọ **dịghị** emetụta ọpụpụ chat/usage.
> `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-ekedo oke nke bypass ahụ.

---

## 9. Njikọ Ngwaọrụ CLI

Na-achịkwa otu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ n'ime PATH sistemụ; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ ndị akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ntinye PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọta binary CLI (e ji akara kọlọn kewaa ha).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(edoghị)_                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ eji agụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị ụzọ zuru ezu nke dị n'ime ụlọ prosesụ ahụ — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e ji bind-mount tinye (ọ bụ otu a ka `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-alaghachi na ndekọ ụlọ. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (ime token ka ọ dị ọhụrụ, data session). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka mbọ ọ bụla ide nhazi CLI daa site na njehie doro anya nke sị "agbanyụrụ ide ihe".                                                               |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, emesịa `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary agent Cursor. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, nchọpụta ga-ebu ụzọ nwalee `agent`, wee laghachi na `cursor` ma ọ bụrụ na nke ahụ arụghị ọrụ.                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na binary 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Mmekọrịta Akpaka nke Profaịlụ CLI

Ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a bụ nke a ga-eji aka họrọ ma agbanyụrụ ha na ndabara. A pụkwara ịgbanwu ha site na
dashboard CLI Code.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model onye na-eweta gasịrị, ọ na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ugbu a. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Codex na-arụ ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-eweta.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta model onye na-eweta gasịrị, ọ na-edegharị faịlụ profaịlụ Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` na akpaghị aka site na katalọgụ dị ugbu a. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Claude na-arụ ọrụ/nke ndabara, auth, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-eweta. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binary ndị dị na host n'ime container ma gwa OmniRoute ebe ha dị:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e mere bind-mount n'ezie nke ụzọ ahụ site na
host — jikọta ya na mounts dịka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee
profaịlụ `host` dị na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ na-adịghị n'ime
home nke onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ bind mount anya, n’ihi na ihe e dere ebe ahụ ga-efunahụ
mgbe e megharịrị container ahụ.

Image ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dịka `USER node`, ya mere `/root` a na-emeghị mount bụ **ọ bụghị** ezigbo
override.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Nke ndabara     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Manye nchọpụta container ka ọ dị ọkụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). A chọrọ ya naanị na runtimes nke nchọpụta akpaka anaghị achọpụta. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka e dee nhazi CLI-tool n’ụzọ container a na-emeghị mount n’agbanyeghị nke ahụ. Ihe ya na ya hà na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.           |

### Ndị enyemaka CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Mgbanwe ndị a na-ahazi omume nke CLI binary `omniroute` n’onwe ya (ọ bụghị nchọpụta
sidecar dị n’elu).

| Mgbanwe                               | Nke ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemụ)_ | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Manye asụsụ mmepụta CLI. Locale BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-aka env vars locale sistemụ (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ stdout/stderr sava na terminal n’ọnọdụ nlekọta. Ya na flag `--log` na `omniroute serve` hà.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth a na-etinye dịka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka na ọrụ 8.12.                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Mgbọrọgwụ home Gemini na-adịgide adịgide; ọsọ otu-ugboro na-eji home nwa oge e kewapụrụ iche.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`      | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge ebere na milliseconds tupu e jiri ike kwụsị CLI child e zigara signal.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ 1 tinyere DATA_DIR akọwapụtara iji mafere faịlụ environment data-directory ndabara nke onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`     | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Oge ngwụcha HTTP kwa nnwale (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → sava.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`     | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI na-eche endpoint ahụike sava tupu o bipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị oge ngwụcha. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite oyi na-adịghị ngwa (dịka Windows). A pụkwara ịtọ ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`         | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr n’oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(edoghị)_  | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory omenala maka nchọpụta plugin CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*`). Mgbe edoghị ya, ndabara bụ `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Naanị CLI — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-erute scanner plugin nke akụkụ sava, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka na ya (ngalaba 2). |

## 10. Njikọ Agent Dị N'ime & MCP

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                             | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka                                | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL akọwapụtara nke ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji ruo OmniRoute. Ọ na-akagbu nchọpụta localhost akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(edoghị)_                                          | Modulu MCP/A2A                                              | Igodo API maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP dị n'ime na oku nka A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edoghị)_                                          | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | NJ igodo maka ikenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edoghị)_                                          | Nke ochie                                                   | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edobe akụkọ ihe mere eme nke ọrụ A2A na ọdụ data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ ga-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Mgbanwe nkwụsị maka atụmatụ nleba anya memory-hits nke A2A. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji mafere kpamkpam nchọgharị ncheta maka ọrụ; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edoghị ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka endpoint nhazi mbụ edere nke Issue Agent na-anọghị n'ịntanetị/mpaghara rụọ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ ọ gwụla ma ị na-eme kpọmkwem usoro ọrụ nhazi mbụ edere na mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(edoghị)_                                          | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka otu ọsọ nhazi mbụ edere nke Issue Agent. A na-amachi ya na oke kachasị dị n'ime; ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara arụnyere mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ na-ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ)_                                  | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Ọnọdụ/profaịlụ CLI maka ọnọdụ ime-anya nke iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-akagbu ọnọdụ nọ n'ọrụ n'ebe nchekwa ọnọdụ mpaghara. Ọ bụ otu ihe na `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend keychain OS `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere ọnọdụ CLI. | `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scope ndị e ji rikoma kewaa: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

A na-amanye ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Etiti oge mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Etiti oge nyocha mmachi ọsọ na oke ojiji nke onye na-eweta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere oge (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke OAuth ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ime mmekọrịta buru ibu; a na-enweta njikọ OAuth otu n'otu iji zere izigara upstream ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge. `0` na-ewepụ nke a (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Etiti oge kacha nta (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke upstream ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ụzọ preflight/monitor nke arịrịọ ọ bụla; ọ na-ekewa oku netwọkụ ndị na-eme n'otu oge ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ nọ n'otu IP ghara izigara upstream ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu oge. E jikọtara ya na ndị na-eweta nnweta oke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (ebe nnweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izipu izugbe `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/wdg.) akabeghị kpuchie ya — a na-esochi ya iche. Ngọngọ karịrị 512KB na runtime igwe ojii ga-anọgide na memori naanị n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Njikọ CLI OAuth (N'ime)

| Mgbanwe             | Ndabara         | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                   | Nkọwa                                      |
| ------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta-akpaka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka njikọ nkwenye CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(edoghị)_      | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nkwenye maka njikọ CLI.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | NJ onye ọrụ maka session njikọ CLI.        |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(edoghị)_      | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.  |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(edoghị)_      | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.   |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(edoghị)_      | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`. |

---

## 11. OAuth Provider Credentials

Asambodo arụnyere n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka ntinye dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke onye na-enye ọrụ ọ bụla.

| Variable                                    | Provider                | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Onye ahịa ọha — ọ dịghị ihe nzuzo achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Gbanwee URI ntụgharị. Nke ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa Muse CLI device-flow ọha. Hapụ ka ọ ghara ịtọlite iji jiri onye ahịa ọha arụnyere n'ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa OAuth nke GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara ọha nke `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe etinyeghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID ntinye onye ahịa GitHub Copilot ezigara na `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version` headers. Nke ndabara bụ `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Igodo API ndabara ejiri site na `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` mgbe enweghị asambodo njikọ ọ bụla dị. Nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Ụzọ omenala gaa na Devin CLI binary (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-edozi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` na URI ntụgharị `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
