## 12. Mgbanwe User-Agent nke Provider

Dochie header `User-Agent` a na-ezigara provider upstream ọ bụla. Executor base class na-ekpebi nke a n'ụzọ dynamic n'oge runtime:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Variable | Uru Ndabara | Mgbe a ga-emelite ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipụ aha tool nke harness ndị ọzọ dịka ha dị gaa na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ ahụ na-aga Anthropic (OAuth nke ya na CLIProxyAPI). Na ndabara, executor na-enye aha ọzọ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ikpebi mgbe niile nye aha tool ndị na-abụghị nke Claude Code (ọ na-eji canonical mapping nke Claude Code ebe nke dị, ma ọ bụghị ya, ọ na-eji PascalCase), ma weghachite aha mbụ ha na response site na `_toolNameMap`, ka a ghara ịjụ harness nwere tool ndị e dere na snake_case dịka third-party client e ji fingerprint mata. Maka debugging naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Dochie ụdị Codex client n'adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Dochie ụdị Claude Code a na-egosi n'adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-amachi ụfọdụ model dabere na uru a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Dochie suffix nwere mkpụrụedemede atọ nke OmniRoute na-agbakwunye na `cc_version=` n'ime billing block nke Claude. Welite ya mgbe ị na-emelite `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — ịkpọgide naanị version ahụ na-egosi otu `version.revision` nke binary ọ bụla dị adị anaghị ewepụta. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Dochie ụdị Copilot CLI a na-egosi n'adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Dochie `clientId` (id ọha) nke social device-code nke Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ: nyocha CRC zuru ezu maka frame ọ bụla na event stream nke Kiro (iji debug stream ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye mgbanwe User-Agent maka provider **ọ bụla** site n'iji pattern `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-emepụta aha env var ahụ n'ụzọ dynamic.

---

## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Oge Nchere

Ụkpụrụ niile dị na **milisekọnd**. A na-eme mkpebi ha n’otu ebe na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Usoro Ọkwa Oge Nchere

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (ntọala zuru ụwa ọnụ nke na-akagbu ndị ọzọ)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (ọkpụkpọ ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n'èzí, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ, atụ aro: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 1260000; 0 na-agbanyụọ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-amachi mgbatị oge njikere na-agbanwe agbanwe, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (ewepụtara ya, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ)
```

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — na-akagbu ndabara nke `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Oge ngwụcha mkpokọta maka arịrịọ HTTP na oku ndị na-eweta ọrụ upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Ogologo oge kachasị nke enweghị data n'etiti bytes raw sitere upstream tupu a kwụsị ya. Ụdịdị nwere echiche gbatịrị agbatị anaghị akwụsịkarị >90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Ogologo ndụ kachasị nke stream SSE na-arụ ọrụ; bytes sitere upstream anaghị eme ka ọ malitegharịa, ọ dịghịkwa adabere na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. E wepụtara ya site na `timeoutMs` kachasị maka otu ụdịdị n'ime ndekọ ahụ (1200000, Codex), tinyere oke mgbakwunye 60000, ka a ghara igbu ụdịdị e nyere ohere iji oge ya niile ka ọ nọ n'etiti azịza. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(agbanyụrụ)_                                                     | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike izipu ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka obi-ọkụ `: keepalive` na metadata trailers `x-omniroute-*`). A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara (#10524) n'ihi na ndị ahịa kwekọrọ na OpenAI n'ụzọ siri ike na-eji JSON.parse ahịrị SSE ọ bụla ma daa mgbe ha hụrụ nkọwa `:`; nke a anaghị emetụta obi-ọkụ `data:`. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji họrọ ịbanye ọzọ. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge iji nata mmemme SSE mbụ na-abụghị ping. Ọ na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Oke windo njikere na-agbanwe agbanwe maka mmemme mbụ n'arịrịọ streaming buru ibu, juputara na ngwaọrụ, ma ọ bụ chọrọ echiche dị elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Mgbanwe mberede maka heuristic `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta kpamkpam — a gaghị ebuli oge ngwụcha njikere na mgbake stream site na request body/headers, nke na-ebelata mmụba oge ngwụcha onye ahịa nwere ike ịchịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Oke windo njikere maka mmemme mbụ n'ọrụ agent `/goal` achọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ ejiri `x-omniroute-agent-goal` manye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Gbanye mgbake stream n'oge na-akpaghị aka maka ọrụ agent `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nhọrọ pụrụ iche maka goal. Nke a nwere ike naanị ỊGBakwunye mgbake n'elu ndabara onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ — ọ naghị akagbu nkwụsị e mere n'ụzọ doro anya site na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/ntọala DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ mmemme SSE `codex.*` na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eme ka `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK daa na 502. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha n'ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Ụgbọ njem app-server Codex a na-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya. Endpoint WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` mpaghara. Mgbe edobere ya tinyere token, a na-ebufe arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa na sidecar kama iji HTTP Responses API. A pụkwara ịhazi ya maka njikọ ọ bụla site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Token ikike/bearer dị n'ime a na-enye app-server. Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Ụzọ faịlụ nwere token ikike app-server (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe edobeghị `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Ndekọ ọrụ nke ntụgharị app-server na-arụ ọrụ n'ime ya. Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Iwu nkwado a na-enyefe na ntụgharị app-server (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Iwu sandbox a na-enyefe na ntụgharị app-server (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edobeghị ya, executor na-eji `workspace-write` dịka ndabara (e mere ka ọ sie ike; na mbụ ọ bụ `danger-full-access`). Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kwado ajụjụ nkwado nke app-server n'onwe ya na-akpaghị aka (mmezu command/faịlụ/ikike n'elu host). Ọ gbanyụrụ na ndabara — a na-ajụ ajụjụ ndị ahụ na-akpaghị aka; nke a anaghị emetụta oku ngwaọrụ harness (ha na-agafe ụzọ passthrough `item/tool/call` dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Nchịkwa dochie maka njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata response headers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Oge nchere kachasị elu maka nkụnyeisi nzaghachi na arịrịọ nkwanye (ms). `0` na-ewepụ oke a; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` nke onye na-eweta akọwapụtara kpọmkwem na-ebute ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge nchere kachasị elu tupu nzaghachi amalite (ms) maka mbọ kpọmkwem ọ bụla na-enweghị proxy. Mgbe oge gwụrụ, ọ na-anwa ọzọ otu ugboro site na socket ọhụrụ; tọọ `0` iji wepụ oke ahụ ma debe omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (nkeji 5)                                                | Oge nchere kacha nta tupu nzaghachi amalite (ms) maka mbọ kpọmkwem na-enweghị proxy nke host ebumnuche ya bụ nkeonwe/LAN/loopback (dịka ọmụmaatụ `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), ka mbido oyi Ollama nke obodo ghara ịbụ nke ndabara 30s dị n'elu kwụsịrị. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ dịka oke kacha nta: oge nchere dị irè bụ nke ka ukwuu n'etiti uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị eziokwu na-ewepụ oke kacha nta obodo ahụ. Nke a anaghị emetụta host ndị dị anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                               | Oke kachasị elu (ms) maka mbọ RETRY nke na-eji socket ọhụrụ mgbe oge nchere mmalite-nzaghachi nke mbọ pooled dị n'elu gwụsịrị (#13703). Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ ahụ tinyelarị mgbaama oge njedebe nke ya (usoro njikọ/model/onye na-eweta/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` e kpebiri); mgbaama ahụ bụ ezigbo oke ma na-amalite mbụ n'ụzọ e zubere, ya mere nke a bụ nchekwa azụ sara mbara kama ịbụ oke kwụ ọtọ — na-enweghị ya, mbọ ọzọ ahụ jiri otu windo mkpirikpi `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` ahụ mbọ pooled ji mee ihe, wee nye model ntụgharị uche nwere TTFB nwayọ ma dị mma njehie 504. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị ala karịa oke kacha nta kwụ ọtọ dị n'elu; mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ ahụ enyeghị mgbaama oge njedebe ọ bụla, mbọ ọzọ ahụ na-edebe oke kacha nta kwụ ọtọ ahụ n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata ahụ nzaghachi zuru ezu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe maka ịmepụta njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge njedebe mgbe socket keep-alive anaghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge njedebe nke proxy akara mkpịsịaka TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-amachi oge ruo mgbe byte mbụ rutere kpọmkwem n'ahụ nke mbufe akara-mkpịsịaka TLS nke wreq-js; naanị `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` enweghị ike ịchọpụta ahụ kwụsịrị n'ihi na ọ na-emezu ozugbo nkụnyeisi rutere (#12656). Mgbe oge gwụrụ, ọ na-akagbu wreq reader ma laghachi na dispatcher kpọmkwem/proxy; `0` na-ewepụ watchdog ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Naanị maka executor OpenCode, nakwa naanị mgbe feature flag `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị n'ọrụ (ọ naghị arụ ọrụ na ndabara): na-amachi oge nchere maka byte mbụ nke ahụ nzaghachi Responses a na-enyefe dịka stream mgbe nkụnyeisi ya rutere (#13484). Stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafee windo a bụ nkwụsị: a na-etinye akaụntụ ahụ n'ọnọdụ cooldown, arịrịọ ahụ wee tụgharịa gaa na akaụntụ na-esote otu ugboro; nkwụsị nke abụọ na-eme ka ọ daa ozugbo. `0` na-ewepụ nchedo ahụ ọbụlagodi mgbe flag dị n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= agbanyụghị)                                                | Naanị maka executor OpenCode: na-amachi oge nchere maka nkụnyeisi nzaghachi upstream na oku Responses a na-enyefe dịka stream mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị — arịrịọ dị n'ahịrị na-akwụsị ichere ma na-aga n'ihu kama ijide windo nkụnyeisi ahụ dum. Naanị oku Responses a na-enyefe dịka stream ka nke a na-emetụta (stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị n'ebe ahụ bụ ahịrị nchere, ọ bụghị mmepụta); nke a anaghị emetụta chat completions na oku ndị na-abụghị stream, windo ahụ na-ebelata naanị oke fetch-start dị irè, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbatị ya. Uru a tụrụ aro mgbe a na-agbanye ya: `30000`. `0` na-ewepụ ya.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Naanị maka executor OpenCode: ugboro ole kwa arịrịọ oke nchere nkụnyeisi dị n'elu nwere ike ịkwaga na akaụntụ na-esote. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-edebe windo nkụnyeisi zuru ezu mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Naanị maka executor OpenCode: dobe arịrịọ ahụ nwa oge site na heartbeat mgbe 429 nwa oge mere ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ mgbe e nwere akara pool-strain ọhụrụ), wee kpọgharịa otu akụkụ nwere oke nke ruru akaụntụ 3 n'usoro kama iziga ya n'otu oge n'ìgwè akaụntụ niile (#13924). Agbanyụghị na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ na-esote kpọmkwem dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Naanị maka executor OpenCode: mgbe ebe nchekwa ọjụjụ proxy wepụrụ akaụntụ niile dị njikere mgbe cooldown gasịrị n'akụkụ replay nke park-and-resume, jiri ha rụọ ọrụ n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ kama ịghara ịkpọgharịa ihe ọ bụla (#14851, ihe sochiri #14750). Agbanyụghị na ndabara: akụkụ replay ahụ na-abụ ihe efu n'ọnọdụ ahụ, dịka ọ dị tupu #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Naanị maka chat nkwanye: mgbe ahụ upstream mbụ kwụsịrị tupu o mepụta event bara uru, mee otu mbọ nke abụọ nwere oke site n'otu ụzọ routing ahụ, jiri otu readiness budget ahụ ma ghara ịta akaụntụ ahụ ahụhụ. Agbanyụghị na ndabara: ahụ mbụ kwụsịrị na-eme ka arịrịọ ahụ daa n'enweghị mbọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Naanị maka executor OpenCode: mgbe 429 sitere n'aka onye na-eweta kewara dịka egress-bucket na akaụntụ na-enweghị proxy n'okpuru ọnọdụ pool gbara ya gburugburu, rịọ connection pool ka ọ họrọ member ọzọ maka mbọ na-esote kama ịnwa otu adreesị egress ahụ ọzọ. Ọ na-ahazi usoro, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-ewepụ ihe: pool gwụchara na-edebe omume dị ugbu a. Agbanyụghị na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ na-esote kpọmkwem dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: gbanwee ụzọ faịlụ pool-strain marker a na-agụ tupu e dọba ya (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Marker ọhụrụ na-adọba ya n’agụghịgharị ọnụ; ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị ma ọ bụ na ọ bụ nke ochie, a ga-alaghachi na burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe nke proxy hop maka arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Duzie Firecrawl web-fetch executor gaa na instance nke ị na-akwado n’onwe gị (API key abụghị iwu ma ọ bụrụ na ọ bụghị na cloud).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe maka arịrịọ ọ bụla nke Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Base URL maka Tavily `/usage` quota fetcher (Oke Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ); a na-eleghara ya anya mgbe ọ na-atụ aka na `api.tavily.com`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a ga-eji base URL nke njikọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Oge njedebe zuru ezu nke arịrịọ server maka bridge ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Oge e nyere iji zipụ response headers site na bridge ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Oge njedebe mgbe njikọ keep-alive nke bridge anaghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Oge njedebe nke raw socket (0 = agbanyụrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Oge amara mgbe SIGTERM/SIGINT gasịrị tupu mmemme ahụ apụ n’ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Uru ndabere nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe edoghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge njedebe (ms) maka preset `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` dị na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Bulie ya maka endpoint ndị na-adịghị ngwa (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịgbanwegharị n’etiti active/error na dashboard. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka uru na-adịghị irè (<1000) ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Oge njedebe fetch pụrụ iche maka relay dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụsịgoro ịzaghachi ga-ada tupu oge njedebe nke client/agent (~30s) ka ndị na-akpọ ya hụ ọdịda metụtara relay kpọmkwem kama ịhụ oge njedebe upstream izugbe. A na-amachi ya na `29000` ka ọ na-eme mgbe niile tupu nke ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Oge nchere retry nke a na-ekekọrịta maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy ndị na-anwalegharị otu ugboro dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwaa ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke absolute JS hard-deadline agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke absolute JS hard-deadline agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Na-agbakwunye "Ị nwere nchọta weebụ arụnyere n’ime ya. Jiri nsonaazụ nchọta zaa ajụjụ ozugbo." na system message nke onye na-akpọ ya (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — Perplexity ka na-eme nchọta, ahịrịokwu ahụ na-apụtakwa na nzaghachi dịka meta-commentary nye coding clients. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachite ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke absolute JS hard-deadline agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ native wreq-js (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya ruo `180000` maka arịrịọ ọ bụla mgbe generation na-ewe ogologo oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara nke absolute JS hard-deadline agbakwunyere n’elu oge njedebe native.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Ọdọ ihe nchọgharị Playwright a na-ekekọrịta maka nkata kuki-weebụ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ binarị `obscura` nke ọdọ ihe nchọgharị na onye mmebe Cloudflare Playground ji dị ka injin bụ isi (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH nke sistemụ mgbe a na-atọpụtaghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(atọpụtaghị)_                                                    | Tụọ aka na Obscura na-agba ọsọ ugbua (`http://host:port`) kama ịmalite nke ọhụrụ; modulu ahụ anaghị achịkwa usoro ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri akọwapụtara maka `obscura serve` a malitere; a na-ahọrọ ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe a na-atọpụtaghị ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Mee ka arịrịọ nkata kuki-weebụ jiri ụzọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); jiri `1` mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL ntọala maka onye mmebe Kimi Web (kimi.ai mba ụwa nke na-eji Connect-RPC) (`kimi-web.ts`); gbanwee ya naanị maka ebe njedebe mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Ebe njedebe nkata zuru ezu maka onye mmebe Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(a chọpụtara na-akpaghị aka)_                                    | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ Chrome/Edge nke sistemụ nwere ike imepụta maka nbanye ihe nchọgharị mmekọrịta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ọ na-anọchi nchọpụta akpaka nke OS ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _mmepụta standalone nke .build/_                                  | Mgbanwe oge-nrụpụta maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke nzọụkwụ idokọta ihe ọnụ mgbe nrụpụta gasịrị na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); ọ bụ maka ngwa nrụpụta, ọ bụghị oge-ngagharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Mgbalị ebumnuche ngwakọta na-eketa oge nchere arịrịọ upstream a haziri (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara fetch). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime ngwakọta,
ndabara ngwakọta, ma ọ bụ mgbanwe onye na-eweta naanị iji mee ka ọdịda gaa na nhọrọ ọzọ mee ngwa ngwa; a na-amachi uru ndị karịrị
oge nchere upstream dị ugbu a ka ha hà nhata oge nchere upstream ahụ.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ oke oge mgbidi dị iche maka ngwakọta niile n'ofe ebumnuche ọdịda gaa na nhọrọ ọzọ.
Hapụ ya ka a ghara ịtọ ya ma ọ bụ tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji nọgide na-emegharị n'enweghị oke (nkwụsị siri ike nke nkeji 10 e dere kpọmkwem na
`comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta ya). Uru dị mma na-anọchi
usoro nchekwa ahụ maka ngwakọta ahụ. Mee ka `comboTimeoutMs` dị ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka ọdịda gaa na nhọrọ ọzọ
ka nwee oge mgbe ebumnuche mbụ dị nwayọ.

### Oke Ihe Nkwụsị Sekit

Nhazi ihe nkwụsị sekit n'ọkwa onye na-eweta. Ndalaba ndabara na-egosipụta uru ndị a gbasawanyere ejirila kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Mgbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi ibe ya maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu circuit breaker ahụ ebido ọrụ.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oge windo nrụpụta-ọzọ (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi ibe ya maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oge windo nrụpụta-ọzọ (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi ibe ya maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oge windo nrụpụta-ọzọ (ms) maka circuit breaker nke onye na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta OAuth niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo na-aga n'ihu nke ngụkọta ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge nchere (ms) ozugbo e ruru oke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụọgụ kachasị eji amụba nrịgo resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                                                                                        |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta API-key niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo na-aga n'ihu nke ngụkọta ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Circuit breaker n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta mpaghara niile abanye n'oge nchere.                                                                                                                   |

| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Oge nchere maka key ọ bụla (ms), nke ntụgharị API-key nwere ọtụtụ key na-etinye mgbe nzaghachi 429 sitere n'elu enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Uru na-abụghị nke ọma ma ọ bụ nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara. |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                      | Nhazi                                                    |
| ------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**       | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                   |
| **Debe oke maka ngụkọta oge stream niile** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)            |
| **Ọdịda-ngwa-ngwa maka API mmepụta**       | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                      |
| **Ụdịdị nwere echiche gbatịrị agbatị**     | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti chunks) |
