Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mafere nbudata ndabere ahụ kpamkpam (ọ naghị egbochi ọrụ, ọ dịghịkwa ebutekarị ọdịda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (24h)                                                            | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | TTL cache maka snapshot ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị na-eweta ọrụ OpenRouter, n'ime milisekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_IMAGE`                        | `redis:7-alpine`                                                            | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Image Redis nke ihe mmalite Redis otu-pịa na-eji. Gbanwee ya ka ọ bụrụ `redis:8-alpine` ma ọ bụ mirror ndekọ nkeonwe dịka achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QDRANT_HOST`                                  | `qdrant`                                                                    | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Aha host nke sidecar Qdrant mgbe `--profile memory` na-arụ ọrụ. Ndabara ahụ na-arụtụ aka na aha ọrụ qdrant dị n'ime netwọkụ; gbanwee ya maka deployment mpụga. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `qdrantEnabled` bụ `true` na koodu (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts:108`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QDRANT_PORT`                                  | `6333`                                                                      | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Port REST nke sidecar Qdrant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QDRANT_GRPC_PORT`                             | `6334`                                                                      | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Port gRPC nke sidecar Qdrant. Ọba akwụkwọ client ndị na-ahọrọ gRPC karịa REST maka ọrụ streaming na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(edoghị ya)_                                                               | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Igodo API nhọrọ maka Qdrant Cloud ma ọ bụ instance on-prem nwere nyocha njirimara. Oghere → anaghị eziga header `api-key`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Aha collection maka embeddings ebe nchekwa mkparịta ụka OmniRoute. A na-emepụta ya na mbido mbụ site na iji dimensions `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QDRANT_EMBEDDING_MODEL`                       | `text-embedding-3-small`                                                    | _(profaịlụ cluster a na-ahọrọ itinye)_                                    | Aha model embedding ndabara e dekọrọ na metadata collection Qdrant. Onye na-eweta ọrụ nke field `embeddingModel` dị na ntọala OmniRoute na-arụtụ aka na ya bụ ya na-emepụta embeddings ndị ahụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`                           | `1536`                                                                      | _(profaịlụ klọsta nhọrọ)_                                                 | Akụkụ vector embedding. Ọ ga-adabarịrị na model ị ji eme embedding (text-embedding-3-small → 1536; ada-002 → 1536; nomic-embed-text → 768).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(profaịlụ klọsta nhọrọ)_                                                 | Izi ezi nke owuwu index HNSW n'oge a na-ewu ya. Nke dị elu = owuwu ji nwayọọ, ọchụchọ na-adị ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ntụgharị akaụntụ onye njikwa nwere ike ịhazi. Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, ọ dịghị otu n'ime klaasị `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` ndị dị n'okpuru ga-akpalite ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ (ọnọdụ gbanyụrụ-ukwu a na-egbochikwa klaasị 429/500/502 ndị agbanyere na ndabara). Ọ na-enye ohere ka front-end na-ahụ maka ya (dịka ngwa desktọpụ VibeProxy) detuo iwu ntụgharị nke ya n'ime injin ịlaghachi n'akaụntụ ọzọ nke backend.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oge nchere (sekọnd) a na-etinye n'akaụntụ e tinyere oke ọnụego mgbe ọrụ upstream enyeghị ntụaka nrụgharị doro anya. `0` = jiri oge nchere ndabara nke injin kama override a kapịrị ọnụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Oyiri nke mmasị front-end "akpado dị ka nke e tinyere oke ọnụego ma ọ bụrụ na enweghị oge nrụgharị".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ịgbanye ịlaghachi azụ maka ọnọdụ ọ bụla n'ihe njehie `429`. Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ma `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` agaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ, kama a ga-ezigara ya onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `429` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ozugbo, na-echekwa omume mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-amịgharị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụta njehie `429` n'ime ya ka ha ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ịgbanye ịlaghachi azụ maka ọnọdụ ọ bụla n'ihe njehie sava `5xx` (ewezuga `502`, nke nwere klaasị nke ya). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, njehie ndị a agaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `5xx` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` achọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-agagharị agagharị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `5xx` n'ime ya ruo mgbe ha ruru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ntọala ndabere maka ọnọdụ ọ bụla nke na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ maka njehie `502` (ọnụ ụzọ na-adịghị mma). Mgbe ọ bụ `false`, `502` anaghịzi akpalite ntụgharị akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `502` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ya ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-agagharị agagharị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `502` n'ime ya ruo mgbe ha ruru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nhọrọ a ga-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya (ndabara bụ GBANYỤRỤ): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, `400` nkịtị (arịrịọ na-adịghị mma) na-akpalitekwa ntụgharị akaụntụ. Nke a na-agbakwụnye naanị — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-egbochi omume engine dị ugbu a ebe `400` nwere ederede gbasara oke ọnụego/oke ojiji ka na-agafe na nke ọzọ n'agbanyeghị flag a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụọgụ njehie `400` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` a chọrọ tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ (a na-ele nke a anya naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Window na-agagharị agagharị (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `400` n'ime ya ruo mgbe ha ruru `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### Onye Nhazi Oge Ikpo Ọkụ Claude

Ikpo ọkụ nke cron na-achị maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị họọrọ isonye, ka e wee jiri obere arịrịọ ahaziri oge mepee window oke ọnụego nke awa 5 kama iji arịrịọ izizi n'ezie mepee ya (#8848). Onye nhazi oge ahụ na-anọgide gbanyụrụ belụsọ ma `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` bụrụ eziokwu **ma** e tinye akara na njikọ ahụ n'ime `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ tọgbọ chakoo pụtara na ọ dịghị ihe a ga-ekpo ọkụ ọbụlagodi mgbe env var ahụ dị ọkụ.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(edoghị → gbanyụrụ)_       | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mgbanwe ukwu maka nhazi warmup. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ọ naghị ele nnukwu ma ọ bụ obere mkpụrụedemede anya, a na-ewepụ oghere ndị dị n'akụkụ). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ mgbe edoghị ya, na-ahapụ nhazi ahụ ka ọ gbanyụọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nkọwapụta cron nwere mpaghara ise maka tick warmup, nke a na-enyocha na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge nrụgharị nke Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere host.                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọnụọgụ njikọ a na-eme warmup ha n'otu oge maka tick ọ bụla. A na-amachi ya n'etiti `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ ga-alaghachi na `3`.                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model a na-eji maka arịrịọ warmup. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị na atụmatụ gị; họrọ model dị ọnụ ala kachasị nke ka nwere ike imepe window ahụ.                                                                      |

### Oge VNC Maka Nbanye-Brọuza & Utuaha Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC dị n'ime container nke a na-eji enweta nzere nbanye-brọuza site na mmekọrịta (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere utuaha `DATA_DIR` ochie. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara VNC na-ezo aka na image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` e tinyere n'ime ya, a na-agbanwekwa ha naanị maka image container ahaziri iche, ports, ma ọ bụ nhazi usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tag image Docker maka container nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ mee ka nke a zoo aka na image ahaziri iche.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binary runtime container a na-eji ebido container VNC (dịka ọmụmaatụ, tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `podman`).                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC/noVNC e kpughere n'ime container ahụ.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container ahụ.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ ndekọ profaịlụ Chromium dị n'ime container ahụ.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ host nke na-edobe profaịlụ nbanye-brọuza na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị n'ihi enweghị ọrụ (ms) tupu ewepụ oge VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (nkeji 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) maka ogologo ndụ otu oge VNC.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke oge VNC nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ichere ka brọuza dị n'ime container dịrị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka iwekọta oge/cookies e jidere mgbe nbanye mechara.                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Flags command-line ndị ọzọ a na-enyefe Chromium dị n'ime container.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọk Docker pụrụ iche nke container nbanye VNC na-esonye na ya (#12571) kama bridge ndabara, ka containers ndị ibe ya ghara iru port bridge CDP ya.                                          |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edoghị)_                                                               | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Utuaha ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị ma `DATA_DIR` ma `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`. Ọ na-achọta cache oge web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

---

## 26. Nnwale & Ngwaọrụ E2E

Ndị a bụ ndị `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji. Ahapụla
uru niile dị n'okpuru n'enweghị nhazi na mwepụta prodọkshọn.

| Mgbanwe                                | Ndabara                                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọnọdụ mmalite E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-eji `INITIAL_PASSWORD` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe onye nchịkwa a na-etinye n'ime gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck mpaghara mgbe Playwright na-agba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanyụọ usoro nyocha ugboro ugboro nke healthcheck token OAuth n'oge ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(edoghị ya)_                                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-eweta ọrụ, nke e ji rikoma kewaa, a ga-ewepụ na nyocha mweghachi token a na-eme tupu oge eruo (dịka `codex,openai`). Ọ bụ ụzọ ọzọ ezubere iche kama ịgbanyụ healthcheck kpamkpam — ndị na-eweta ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpụmkpụ ga-anọgide na-emelite, ebe ndị na-eweta cascade ga-emeghachi omume naanị mgbe ọ dị mkpa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                                           | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ healthcheck na stdout nke Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mafere mwube prodọkshọn Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (njikarịcha CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                              | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Mafere hook iwepụ OmniRoute (CI na-eji ya edobe `node_modules` ka ọ ghara ịgbanwe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                                         | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge nchere (ms) ka sava nwee ezi ọnọdụ tupu ịgba ule ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke ngwaọrụ smoke Electron na-atụ anya ka ngwa a kwakọtara nye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ngụkọta oge ngwụcha (ms) tupu ngwaọrụ smoke kwụsị ịnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge nkwụsị (ms) mgbe ibe ahụ kwụsịrị ibudata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(akpaka)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke faịlụ executable Electron a kwakọtara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                                       | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ndekọ data maka ịgba ule smoke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji chekwaa ndekọ data smoke mgbe arụmọrụ ahụ gachara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ ndekọ Electron ozugbo na stdout n'oge arụmọrụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa site n'iji otu ndekọ data ahụ ma gosi na mmalite nke abụọ họọrọ draịva SQLite nke sistemụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọgharị PATH)_                               | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Dochie ụzọ faịlụ binari Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Usoro ntụgharị asụsụ dọkụmentị

Nke `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji (iwu `npm run i18n:run`).
Agbanwebeghị mgbanwe ise niile na ndabara — tọọ ha na `.env` naanị na kọmputa
ndị kwesịrị inwe ike ịrụ onye ntụgharị dọkụmentị ahụ.

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL ntọala kwekọrọ na OpenAI maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ.                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Token Bearer maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ (anaghị edekọ ya ma ọlị).                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(atọbeghị)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | NJ model, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`      | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Oge ngwụcha arịrịọ ọ bụla na milisekọnd.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Arịrịọ ntụgharị asụsụ ndị na-aga n'otu oge mgbe a na-arụ ọrụ n'elu ọtụtụ faịlụ / mpaghara asụsụ. |

## 27. Nri Radar (Nnabata n'Onwe Gị)

Mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED na-achịkwa (agbanyụrụ na ndabara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara
a na-agbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Ngbanwe anọ mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ iji dochie ntọala maka nri a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara,
yana usoro igodo ndị nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị uru ndabara nke na-eduga na
ogwe ọrụ nzuzo nke onye nwe ya. Lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka akwụkwọ zuru ezu
banyere modul ahụ na
[usoro ịgbalite ya site na mmalite ruo na njedebe na nhazi a na-eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

A naghị eji environment variable ahazi ngwa ọgụgụ ọkwa Home/Changelog izugbe,
ọ dịghịkwa adabere na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-eji naanị GET agụ URL `news.json`
nke repository ọha, nke akọwapụtara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts`;
ID nke ihe a jụrụ ka dị na nchekwa mpaghara nke ihe nchọgharị.

| Variable                      | Ndabara                                      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL ntọala nke katalọgụ, ntụnye, onyinye ndị nkwado, na nri Intel ndị a bịanyere aka na ha iche iche na-ekekọrịta. Dochie ya ka ọ na-atụ aka na ọrụ a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara e debere kpọmkwem)_          | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Igodo ọha Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) e ji enyocha mbinye aka nri sitere na nri ahaziri iche.                                                                                            |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye nyere aka" na-emepe (usoro ịnata igodo onye nkwado site na GitHub OAuth).                                                                                     |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Kwado ọrụ ahụ" na-emepe (ibe ịkwụ ụgwọ/atụmatụ).                                                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ nzuzo nke ogwe ọrụ maka naanị onye nwe ya. A chọrọ HTTPS, ma e wezụga maka nnyefe SSH loopback nke HTTP; uru edoghị ma ọ bụ nke na-ezighi ezi anaghị emepụta ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla.           |

---

## Nnyocha: Variable E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghịzi Arụ Ọrụ

Variable ndị a pụtara na ụdị `.env.example` ndị gara aga, mana ha **enweghị ntụaka runtime ọ bụla** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụla ha:

| Mgbanwe                                                                                                                                                                         | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla anaghị agụ ya. SQLite bụ naanị draịva a na-akwado — ọ dịghị mkpa ịhọrọ nke ọ bụla.                                    |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Ọ dị na dọkụmentị ochie/ụkpụrụ env mana anaghị eji ya n'oge arụmọrụ. Ọ nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ-instance n'ọdịnihu.              |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. E nweghị mmachi e ji aka tinye n'ogo nchekwa data.                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụla nhicha faịlụ ochie.                                                           |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị faịlụ CLI binary).                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | E zoghị aka na ha ebe ọ bụla n'ime koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                          |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ ha na v3.8.0. Runtime anaghịzi agụ env vars ndị a — nzere na-abịa site na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Kiro dị na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — toggle ya enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.                                         |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na stubs API key ndị ọzọ anaghị eji eme ihe na v3.8.0.                                                                              |

### Ndozi Uru Ndabara

| Mgbanwe                   | Uru `.env.example` Ochie | Ndabara Koodu N'ezie | Ihe Edoziri                                                   |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |

### Mmegharị config OpenCode (ngwaọrụ ad-hoc)

`scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji ya emegharị `opencode.json`
nwere uru `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi, nke e wetara site na instance
OmniRoute na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ndị a achọrọ maka arụmọrụ nkịtị — script a
bụ naanị ngwaọrụ ndị mmepe.

| Mgbanwe            | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | URL ntọala nke instance OmniRoute a ga-ajụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API maka nkwenye njirimara megide endpoint OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OPENCODE_API_KEY`.            |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) nke a na-ede n'ime `opencode.json` e mepụtaghachiri. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OMNIROUTE_KEY`. |

### Ngwa nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị (ngwa ad-hoc)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị, na-eji ya.
Achọghị ya maka ọrụ nkịtị — ọ bụ naanị ngwa ndị nrụpụta.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Ozi nzere JSON nke onye njikwa nyere maka provider nke CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị na-anwale (a na-eji `JSON.parse` atụgharị ya). Hapụ ya n'edoghị maka nnwale na-enweghị mmejuputa. |

### Oge Nchọgharị VNC

`src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji ha ahazi oge Chromium na-enweghị interface eserese nke dabere na Docker maka ndị provider akpaaka nchọgharị. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — e gosiri ndabara n'okpuru.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi                                   | Nkọwa                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na binary Docker e ji emepụta container VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Image Docker maka container VNC Chromium.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(flags arụnyere n'ime)_       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Arụmụka CLI Chromium ndị ọzọ a na-ebufe na nchọgharị dị n'ime container.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC dị n'ime container.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ profaịlụ dị n'ime container.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị n'akụkụ host maka profaịlụ nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe ịnọ nkịtị (ms) tupu a chịkọta oge VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị nke session (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ session VNC kachasị nwere ike ịrụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchọgharị ịdị njikere (ms).                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchịkọta/nhicha (ms).                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche container na-esonyere (#12571), kama bridge ndabara. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Ndekọ maka nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke session eriri Notion.               |

### Nkwenye njirimara ọrụ ime

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Token inline maka nkwenye njirimara site n'otu ọrụ gaa n'ọzọ na management plane.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nwere token ọrụ ime (ọ ka mma n'ime container; ọ na-akagbu mgbanwe inline). |

### Ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara   | Nkọwa                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mafere iweta ọnụọgụ OpenRouter nke provider ọ bụla maka ime ka katalọgụ baa ọgaranya. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL cache (ms) maka ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter e wetara.                                                           |

### Njikọ Redis agbakwunyere n'ime

| Mgbanwe                     | Ndabara     | Nkọwa                                                               |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adreesị njikọ maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                          |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Ntọgharị nwere oke OmniRoute maka adreesị njikọ Redis agbakwunyere. |

## 24. Ihe ndị agbakwunyere na mwepụta v3.8.50

E webatara ntọala ndị a mgbe e mechara snapshot nkwekọrịta gburugburu ebe obibi gara aga.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmiri                                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oge kachasị a ga-echere oghere nnabata nkata dị arọ tupu e weghachi `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ; obere nchere nwere oke na-ahazi mwakpo arịrịọ ndị agent n'usoro kama iweghachi `503` ozugbo. `0` na-eweghachi ọjụjụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oke bytes ndị e tinyere n'ahịrị maka nchere nnabata: ọ na-amachi mkpokọta bytes nke body echekwara na buffer n'ofe process niile ka nchere ahụ ghara ịbawanye heap (#4380). Nchere ndị gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` nke enwere ike ịnwale ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Nke a bụ no-op a kwụsịrị ịkwado kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ lane nnabata nke session ọ bụla ma jiri otu budget maka process niile dochie ha. A ka na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Nke a bụ no-op a kwụsịrị ịkwado kemgbe #10110: e wepụrụ lane nnabata nke session ọ bụla ma jiri otu budget maka process niile dochie ha. A ka na-anabata ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (agbanyụrụ)            | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Lane nnabata virtual nke runtime na-agbanwe onwe ya (#9654): isi switch maka adaptive gate nke tenant ọ bụla (usoro 2). Ọ dị iche na vars lane nke connection ọ bụla a kwụsịrị ịkwado n'elu (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, ndị bụ no-op kemgbe #10110). Ọ bụkwa feature flag dị na dashboard nke nwere otu aha ahụ; env var na-akarị override nke dashboard; ọ chọrọ restart. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Na-amachi ogologo oge oku run-now ga-echere job na-aga n'ihu tupu ọ malite run e tinyere n'ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ browser dị ọhụrụ site na session Chrome CDP nke account ọ bụla; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-echekwa session Adobe ndị a rụziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`; tọọ `0` maka ọnọdụ dị naanị na memory.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oghere oge kacha nta n'etiti submission ọ bụla nke Adobe Firefly generate.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oge nkwụsị ọzọ mgbe submission Adobe atọ ọ bụla gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Ọnọdụ true-headless maka debugging naanị; Adobe colligo na-ajụkarị session ihe ize ndụ sitere na ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | nchọpụta-akpaka            | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Chrome executable path zuru oke a na-etinye ma ọ bụrụ na achọrọ ya, nke executors na-achịkwa browser na-eji mgbe nchọpụta-akpaka nke platform ezughị oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(edoghị)_                 | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token BotFather nke na-eme ka webhook mbata rụọ ọrụ ma na-etinye mbinye aka na Mini App `initData`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(edoghị)_                 | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Ihe nzuzo nkekọrịta e debanyere site na `setWebhook` ma nyochaa megide header `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` na nnyefe webhook ọ bụla. A chọrọ ya maka path webhook; ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a ga-eji 503 jụ nnyefe webhook.                                                                                                                                           |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model a na-eji maka nzaghachi nkata Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Override URL ntọala Bot API maka proxy ma ọ bụ sava Bot API a na-akwado n'onwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Timeout na milliseconds maka oku Bot API na-apụ apụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (agbanyere)            | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Tọọ `0` ka a ghara imepụta tarball `.tar.gz` mgbe a na-akwadebe pack ML/browser nhọrọ maka osisi standalone Electron (a ka ga-emepụta directory pack na `optional-packs.index.json`). Workflow mwepụta desktop na-eji nke a iji belata nha artifact a na-ebugo.                                                                                                        |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED`          | `false`                    | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`                                                       | Na-eme ka ndebanye route onyonyo subscription xAI rụọ ọrụ; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka path onyonyo API-key ghara ịgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                    | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Oge cooldown maka key ọ bụla na milliseconds mgbe nzaghachi `429` na-enweghị ntụnye `Retry-After` bara uru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`           | agbanyere                  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts`                                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off`, `0`, ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ ndekọ na ojiji calibration estimator dị na memory.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(nke ime)_                | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Override signal-handler nke standalone-server maka ojiji ime; launcher na-edobe ma na-ehichapụkarị ya kama ndị na-arụ ọrụ ịhazi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Ntọala ndabara zuru ụwa ọnụ maka browser headless na tunnel tool na-apụ apụ. Uru connection edobere na dashboard nwere ikike karịa.

| Ngbanwe                                 | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi                                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara kpọmkwem maka ojiji npm, systemd na PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Nhọrọ ndabere nkịtị maka ụzọ Chrome/Chromium akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ebe njedebe CDP nke ime; Docker na-eji sidecar dị na ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(edobeghị)_                   | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Ọ bụrụ na edobere ya, arịrịọ ọ bụla ezigara CDP proxy sidecar ga-ejikọrịrị uru a na heda `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Enweghị uru, proxy ahụ ga-ebufe arịrịọ na-enweghị nyocha njirimara — mgbe ahụ, naanị ikewapụ netwọkụ Compose `chatgpt-web-codex-net` na-enye nchebe. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | ID ọwara OpenAI zuru ụwa ọnụ maka okirikiri ngwa Codex mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Igodo runtime ọwara zuru ụwa ọnụ; egosila ya n'ime ndekọ mgbe ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Aha ziri ezi nke njikọ omenala ChatGPT e mepụtara ọhụrụ maka àkwà mmiri MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ndekọ pụrụ iche maka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị, broker na ọwara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, a na-esere onyonyo nyocha ihe nchọgharị n'ebe nyocha ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary launcher na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(nchọpụta-akpaghị aka)_       | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary runtime Bun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Nhọrọ ndabere legacy maka `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; nhazi ọhụrụ na-eji aha kanọnịkal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

---

## Àkwà Mmiri OmniConductor

Onye nnata SSE na-adịte aka nke na-egosipụta ọrụ hub OmniConductor n’ime A2A TaskManager mpaghara (`src/lib/conductor/`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — àkwà mmiri ahụ na-amalite naanị mgbe edobere `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Ọ bụ naanị maka akụkụ sava: token hub ahụ agaghị eru na ihe nchọgharị ma ọlị.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                 | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL ntọala nke hub OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya = agbanyụrụ àkwà mmiri ahụ.        |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ihe nzere hub maka iyi SSE — mepụta peer nke ụdị `spokesperson` na hub ahụ (`POST /v1/peers`, onye nchịkwa).            |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Ihe nzere maka inyefe ọrụ A2A→hub na-abata (`POST /v1/tasks`); ọ na-alaghachi iji `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` mgbe edoghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL ntọala nke ọrụ spokesperson (Faro) dị n’azụ proxy nkata dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                           |

### Nhazi oge na-eburu oke n’uche

`open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji nke a maka nyocha mmefu token tupu arịrịọ. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — omume routing ndabara anaghị agbanwe mgbe edoghị ya.

| Mgbanwe                            | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, mafere njikọ ndị mmefu token ha n’ime windo ọ bụla (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebụl `provider_quota_state`) na-enweghị ike ịkwụ ụgwọ atụmatụ ọnụ ahịa arịrịọ tupu izipu ya. Kwe ka ọ gaa n’ihu mgbe ahazighị mmefu ọ bụla.                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(on)_  | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Nhazi nzaghachi nke ihe atụmatụ token context-guard (#14931): EMA maka (provider, model, tools present) ọ bụla nke `usage.prompt_tokens` / atụmatụ, nke amachibidoro n’etiti 0.15–2.0, naanị n’ime ebe nchekwa. `off`, `0` ma ọ bụ `false` na-agbanyụ ndekọ na itinye ya n’ọrụ. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false` | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Na-edebanye aha ụzọ onyonyo `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli`. Ọ bụ akara atụmatụ dashboard nwere otu aha ahụ; override DB na-emeri uru a.                                                                                                                                      |
